Conclusion: 通过结合大规模多模态数据集与针对触觉稀疏性设计的STAR训练方案，机器人能够实现出色的真实世界多指灵巧操作性能。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires coordinated multi-finger control and effective tactile feedback, yet learning these capabilities remains challenging due to the lack of large-scale real-world data and the difficulty of extracting effective representations from sparse tactile signals. We build a robot platform and teleoperation system to collect a 200-hour bimanual dexterous manipulation dataset with synchronized visual, tactile, and language annotations, comprising 10,576 trajectories across 65 tasks, 69.5% of which involve dexterous multi-finger manipulation. We further propose STAR, an integrated training recipe for vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) models that addresses the spatial, temporal, and informational sparsity of tactile signals through visual-tactile joint pre-training, sparse-global tactile token representation, and sparse future tactile prediction. Trained on this dataset, STAR achieves a 61% average success rate across four real-world tasks with 100 post-training trajectories per task, demonstrating dexterous performance under task-specific post-training.

</details>


### [222] [A Hierarchical Coverage Path Planning Algorithm for Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12595)
*Zongyuan Shen,Haodong Liu,Gao Wang,Hongbin Ma,Yaming Ou,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对未知环境的在线覆盖路径规划算法，通过增量式分解树和全局与局部规划相结合的方法，实现了高效的未知区域自主覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中进行高效的覆盖路径规划是一个挑战，传统的离线算法无法适应动态发现的障碍物，因此需要一种能够在导航过程中根据新获取的环境信息实时调整轨迹的在线算法。

Method: 提出了一种基于增量式分解树的在线覆盖路径规划算法。该算法将未知搜索区域逐步分解为子区域并组织成树状结构，通过维护全局覆盖巡回路线并利用局部规划器在选定的子区域内生成覆盖动作用于导航。

Result: 通过复杂场景下的高仿真模拟评估，结果表明与三种基线算法相比，该算法在路径长度和重叠率方面显著提高了覆盖效率。

Conclusion: 该算法能够有效地在未知环境中进行在线覆盖路径规划，通过层级子区域管理和动态轨迹调整，显著提升了机器人的覆盖效率和对未知环境的适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents an online coverage path planning algorithm for unknown environments. During navigation, the initially unknown search area is progressively decomposed into disconnected subareas as new obstacle information is acquired and coverage proceeds. These subareas are organized in an incrementally constructed decomposition tree that preserves their hierarchical parent-child relationships. Based on this tree, a global coverage tour is maintained and updated online by prioritizing newly generated child subareas according to their exploration states and distances from the robot. A local planner then generates coverage motions within each selected subarea, allowing the robot to adapt its trajectory as the environment is gradually revealed. Its performance is evaluated via high-fidelity simulations in complex scenarios. The results show improved coverage efficiency in terms of path length and overlap ratio in comparison to three baseline algorithms.

</details>


### [223] [Quantifying Spectral Differences in Vehicle Between Production Autonomous and Human-Driven Vehicles Across Driving Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12609)
*Peiyi Fang,Xiangyu Li,Yonglin Weng,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究利用真实世界数据集和频域分析框架，量化并揭示了量产自动驾驶汽车（PAV）与人工驾驶汽车（HV）在不同驾驶场景下的运动学特性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖仿真或低级ACC系统，且多使用时域指标，难以反映高-level自动驾驶汽车在真实复杂多变场景下的频域交通动力学特征。

Method: 采用包含四个PAV平台的真实世界数据集，开发了一个频域分析框架，将运动学信号转换到频域提取谱特征，并基于核密度估计和Wasserstein距离比较PAV与HV的差异。

Result: 研究发现明显的场景依赖型PAV-HV光谱差异。例如，跟车时的速度相关差异始终小于巡航时，而雨天会比晴天显著放大加速度相关的差异。

Conclusion: 该研究突出了多场景评估的必要性，并证明了频域分析在表征真实世界条件下PAV与HV运动学差异方面的价值。

Abstract: Differences in vehicle kinematic characteristics between production autonomous vehicles (PAVs) and human-driven vehicles (HVs) have been limitedly investigated by empirical studies. Most recent studies rely on simulation-based models, while some further investigate low-level adaptive cruise control (ACC) systems in controlled experiments. These methods commonly adapt some time-domain metrics to characterize PAV-HV differences across limited driving conditions. However, current PAVs equipped with high-level autonomous driving systems generate driving behaviors in a black box using data-driven models. These fundamentally different mechanisms for generating behaviors may produce distinct kinematic characteristics in traffic. More importantly, these time-domain metrics cannot reflect frequency-related traffic dynamics across different driving scenarios. Thus, this study adapted a real-world PAV dataset with four PAV platforms and developed a frequency-domain framework to quantify kinematic differences between PAVs and HVs across diverse driving scenarios, including varying driving states, lighting, weather, and vehicle densities. The framework transforms kinematic signals into the frequency domain and extracts spectral features, and then compares these features between PAVs and HVs based on kernel density estimation and Wasserstein distance. The results reveal clear scenario-dependent PAV-HV spectral differences. Specifically, speed-related differences were consistently smaller during car-following than cruising, while rainy conditions consistently enlarged acceleration-related differences compared with clear conditions. These findings highlight the necessity of multi-scenario evaluations and demonstrate the value of frequency-domain analysis for characterizing PAV-HV kinematic differences under real-world conditions.

</details>


### [224] [ProClosure: Hierarchical Room-Object Assignment using Progressive Boundary Closure from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12614)
*Vinoth Kumar Muthuraj,Soumyadeep Banik,Kushal Sharma,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Progressive Boundary Closure的方法，用于从单目RGB视频中恢复3D场景图的房间层级结构，解决了传统方法难以区分门洞与未观测墙壁的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景图中物体与房间的正确归属对机器人任务至关重要，但由于建图时的视场限制，墙壁的不完整观测常常导致房间被错误合并，现有方法无法有效区分未观察到的墙壁和真实的门洞。

Method: 引入Progressive Boundary Closure方法，利用SLAM前端和开放词汇分割器获取点云和轨迹，通过逐步向内加厚边界并在自由空间封闭时冻结区域来恢复房间，同时利用视线分析将对象分配到正确的房间。

Result: 在HM3D-Semantics数据集的10层场景测试中，该方法恢复了74个房间（基线为44个），将房间的F1分数从0.741提升至0.890（IoU 0.25），对象到房间的ARI从0.488提升至0.696。

Conclusion: 该方法通过渐进式边界闭合和视线分析，有效解决了单目RGB视频重建中房间分割和物体归属的难题，显著提升了3D场景图的准确性。

Abstract: A 3D scene graph groups objects into rooms. When a robot is asked to fetch an object from the kitchen, that grouping is what tells it where to look. An object recorded in the wrong room is not retrievable by a query naming the correct room. We introduce Progressive Boundary Closure, which recovers room layer from a monocular RGB video. A SLAM front end and an open-vocabulary segmenter supply a structural point cloud, camera trajectory and object tracks. The cloud is rasterised into a top-down map, rooms are recovered from it, and each object takes the room holding most of its extent. The difficulty lies in the map itself. Walls are recorded only where the camera looked, so a gap in the boundary may be a doorway or a stretch of wall that was never observed; nothing distinguishes the two. Prior methods treat both as passages, merging rooms that should remain separate. We observe that both require the same treatment: a room should not extend across either, so both are closed and need not be distinguished. Such an opening closes under a small amount of boundary growth, and few sightlines cross it, so points in different rooms rarely see one another. We use the first to recover rooms and the second to assign objects to them. Rooms are obtained by Progressively thickening the boundary inward and freezing each free-space region once it becomes enclosed, so every opening seals at its own scale rather than at a radius fixed in advance. Camera poses are used as seeds, which removes the sampling heuristic and makes the segmentation deterministic. Over 10 floors of 6 HM3D-Semantics scenes, scored against HOV-SG on identical top-down maps, we recover 74 rooms for 72 annotated regions (HOV-SG: 44), raising room F_1 from 0.741 to 0.890 at IoU 0.25 at some cost in precision, and object-to-room ARI from 0.488 to 0.696 (p=0.002, ahead on every floor).

</details>


### [225] [Online Material Estimation for Conditioned Diffusion Policy in Shaping Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12634)
*Ryunosuke Yamada,Tomohiro Motoda,Yukiyasu Domae,Tokuo Tsuji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件扩散策略，用于解决不同材质可变形线性对象（DLO）的形状控制问题，通过在线估计材质标签来生成适应性的操作动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于DLO的变形行为随材质属性（如刚度和弹性）变化，单一策略在面对相同目标形状时需要为不同物体生成不同的动作序列，这对模仿学习构成了挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于在线估计材质标签条件的扩散政策。利用循环估计网络从多视角图像和机器人关节状态的时间序列中预测被抓取物体的材质标签，并在每个推理步骤中以此条件化扩散策略。

Result: 在480个真实机器人演示（涵盖4种材质和3个任务）上的实验表明，所提出的无需先验材质信息的策略达到了60.8%的成功率，匹配了使用真实地面材质标签策略的性能（60.0%），显著优于仅基于任务的策略（45.8%）。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效在线提取材质相关信息并条件化扩散策略，实现对不同材质可变形线性对象的精准形状控制。

Abstract: Shape control of deformable linear objects (DLOs) is challenging for imitation learning because deformation behavior varies with material properties such as stiffness and elasticity, so a single policy must generate different action sequences for different objects even when the goal shape is identical. We propose a diffusion policy conditioned on material labels that are estimated online during manipulation. A recurrent estimation network predicts the material label of the grasped object from the time series of multi-view images and robot joint states, and the predicted label conditions the diffusion policy at every inference step. We collected 480 real-robot demonstrations covering four DLO materials and three groove-placement tasks, and compared per-material specialist policies, a task-conditioned policy without material labels, a policy conditioned on ground-truth material labels, and the proposed policy. Conditioning on ground-truth material labels improved the average success rate from 45.8% to 60.0% over the task-only policy, and the proposed policy reached 60.8% without any prior material information, matching the policy given ground-truth labels. A post-hoc analysis shows that the estimator extracts material-related information from the manipulation observations and that the diffusion policy responds to the resulting conditioning signal, while the one pronounced failure case is associated with persistent confusion between two similar materials.

</details>


### [226] [Breaking the Vision-Action Shortcut: Latent Interface Training for Generalizable Robotics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12641)
*Jianman Lin,Shailesh Shailesh,Zhongyi Luo,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了隐蔽接口训练（LIT）框架，通过两阶段训练策略解决机器人基础模型中的视觉-动作捷径问题，从而提高模型在视觉分布偏移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型在视觉分布偏移下性能往往会下降，因为它们容易利用训练分布中与动作相关的任务无关视觉捷径，这破坏了模型在分布变化时的泛化能力。

Method: 提出Latent Interface Training（LIT）框架：第一阶段训练一个无视觉输入的空间-目标条件动作先验，学习目标导向的动作生成；第二阶段引入一个通过姿态监督的隐式接口，聚合视觉与语义表征，作为动作专家的唯一视觉调节路径。

Result: 在四个视觉-语言-动作和世界-动作架构上，LIT将LIBERO-Plus的整体成功率提升了3.87-10.70个百分点，并在面对未见过的相机配置、光照变化和干扰物等真实世界评估中，成功率提升了13.30-16.70个百分点。

Conclusion: LIT通过约束视觉信息在动作生成中的使用并保留任务相关的空间信息，有效减轻了视觉-动作捷径，显著增强了机器人基础模型在视觉分布偏移下的泛化性能。

Abstract: Robot foundation models achieve strong in-distribution performance but often degrade under visual distribution shifts. When learning to generate actions from pretrained visual representations, models may exploit task-irrelevant visual cues that correlate with demonstrated actions within the training distribution. Such vision-action shortcuts can undermine generalization when these correlations change under distribution shifts. Mitigating these shortcuts requires constraining how visual information is used for action generation while preserving task-relevant spatial information. We propose Latent Interface Training (LIT), a framework-agnostic two-stage strategy that first establishes a spatial-goal-conditioned action prior without images, then constrains visual conditioning through a pose-supervised latent interface. Stage 1 trains the action expert to generate action chunks conditioned on language, robot state, and each demonstrated chunk's terminal SE(3) end-effector pose, learning goal-directed action generation independently of visual cues. Stage 2 introduces a latent interface that aggregates visual and semantic representations and serves as the pretrained action expert's only visual conditioning pathway. The interface is supervised to reconstruct the terminal pose previously used to condition Stage 1, encouraging it to retain the goal-relevant spatial information needed for action generation. Across four vision-language-action and world-action architectures (Pi0.5, MolmoAct2, FAST-WAM, and ImageWAM), LIT improves overall LIBERO-Plus success by 3.87-10.70 percentage points while preserving or improving average LIBERO success. Real-world evaluations show 13.30-16.70 percentage-point gains in success aggregated across three tasks under unseen camera configurations, lighting variations, and distractors.

</details>


### [227] [Size Doesn't Matter: Material-State Reinforcement Learning for Excavator Transferable Soil Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12677)
*Lennart Werner,Pol Eyschen,Sean Costello,Pierluigi Micarelli,Andrei Cramariuc,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于强化学习和GPU并行物质点法（MPM）土壤模拟的方法，实现了能够利用铲斗多个接触面进行复杂土方作业（如回填、压实等）的自主挖掘机控制策略，并在真实重型挖掘机和小型机器人上成功部署与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主土方系统大多局限于挖掘和倾倒，依赖启发式模型且缺乏对土壤力学的考虑，人类操作员则能灵活使用铲斗的所有面进行复杂的变形土壤作业，为了克服这一短板，研究者希望开发出具备土壤状态感知能力并能利用多接触面操作的通用控制策略。

Method: 使用强化学习结合GPU并行化的物质点法（MPM）粒子模拟器进行训练，控制器根据土壤状态（如形状和紧实度）进行条件化；策略在归一化的末端执行器空间中运行，并通过标定机器接口部署到不同尺度的物理机器上。

Result: 通过一台11.5吨的液压挖掘机自主构建了一个42米长、2.1米高的路堤，执行了201次无故障、无需重试或人工干预的策略冲程；在直接对比中，自主控制器的作业速度与专家操作员相当，且产生更高、更一致的路堤。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将基于强化学习和土壤力学模拟的控制策略迁移到真实物理机器上，证明了材料状态感知及多接触面工具操作在复杂土方任务中的高效性与可行性。

Abstract: Earthmoving tasks such as excavation, backfilling, or embankment construction require deliberate repositioning of deformable soil. For these tasks, human operators use all shovel faces, while autonomous systems so far are limited to excavation and dumping. Current methods often rely on heuristic models but do not incorporate soil mechanics. We address this shortcoming by using Reinforcement Learning in a GPU-parallelized Material Point Method particle simulation. Our controllers are conditioned on material state such as shape and compactness, enabling skills that use multiple contact faces of the tool and displace material both inside and outside of the shovel. To use the same learned weights across machines, our policies operate in a normalized end-effector space and are deployed through a calibrated machine interface. We evaluate this calibrated transfer on an 11.5t hydraulic excavator and a 500g tabletop robot. We validate performance through autonomous construction of a 42m long, 2.1m high embankment in 45min, executing 201 individual policy strokes without failure, retry, or operator intervention. In a direct comparison, the autonomous controller matches an expert operator's progression speed and produces a higher, more consistent embankment. Additional qualitative backfilling and compaction experiments demonstrate the material-state awareness and calibrated transfer across machines.

</details>


### [228] [Improving Imitation Learning Efficiency for Manipulation through Geometric Prior Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12721)
*Shogo Iwakata,Tomohiro Motoda,Ryosuke Yamada,Koshi Makihara,Ryoichi Nakajo,Keitaro Tanaka,Masaki Murooka,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Shigeo Morishima,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于极简几何视觉预训练数据集的方法，用于提升机器人操作策略在样本稀缺时的微调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模仿学习策略在应用到新任务时需要收集大量示范并重新训练，而大规模机器人数据集成本高昂，数据增强也需要重复训练。因此，本研究旨在探索如何在不收集任务特定数据的前提下，以极低成本为策略提供有用的几何先验。

Method: 构建了一个自动生成的几何视觉预训练数据集，场景仅包含平面、物体和用立方体表示的手（以避免形态限制），且无纹理和背景，专注于手与物体的几何关系；并在3种仿真机器人的15个任务以及3个真实世界任务上使用ACT进行评估。

Result: 在许多机器人与任务的组合中，使用该几何先验进行微调在训练早期阶段比从头训练取得了更高的成功率，且仅需少量的任务示范。

Conclusion: 即使是高度简化的几何场景，也能为策略提供有效的初始化，在任务数据有限的情况下实现跨机器人和真实世界任务的有效迁移。

Abstract: Applying an imitation learning policy to a new manipulation task usually requires collecting new demonstrations and retraining the model, which makes sample efficiency a practical concern. Pretraining on large-scale robot datasets is effective in this respect, but such datasets are costly to collect and train on, while data augmentation techniques typically require a new round of data generation and retraining for each task. A complementary question is what useful prior can be provided to a policy at negligible cost before any task-specific data are collected. In this study, we construct a geometric visual pretraining dataset in which each scene contains only a plane, an object, and a hand, and trajectories are generated automatically. The scenes contain neither textures nor backgrounds; pretraining primarily exposes the policy to the geometric relationship between the hand and the object. Furthermore, representing the hand as a cube avoids tailoring the dataset to a specific robot morphology. We evaluate this geometric prior using ACT on three simulated robots across five manipulation tasks each, as well as on three real-world robot tasks. Across many of these robot--task combinations, fine-tuning from the geometric prior achieves higher success rates in the early stages of training than training from scratch while using only a small number of task demonstrations. These results suggest that even highly simplified geometric scenes can provide a useful initialization that transfers across robots and to real-world tasks when task data are limited.

</details>


### [229] [Control Architecture for Safe Grasping of Fragile Objects Using a Coarse Position-Controlled Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12737)
*Marko Pavlic,Moritz Geier,Timo Markert,Darius Burschka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何感知的力/力矩接触估计方法与自适应导纳控制器结合的方案，用于实现机器人在未知物体上的安全、自适应抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化环境中需要安全地抓取未知物体而不损坏它们，这类似于人类通过感知和快速调整抓取力来实现的操作，因此需要自适应的柔顺交互策略来应对不确定的物体属性和抓取不稳定性。

Method: 提出了一种针对粗略位置控制夹爪的几何感知力/力矩接触估计方法，并结合了自适应导纳控制器，实现在线估计期望接触力以保持稳定接触。

Result: 对不同大小、形状、刚度和重量的物体进行的实验表明，该算法不仅能防止打滑，还能施加最小的力来安全抓取物体，同时避免过度变形。

Conclusion: 该方法使机器人在处理具有未知属性的物体时，能够实现柔顺且稳定的抓取，有效防止打滑并避免施加过大的力或造成物体过度变形。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly used in unstructured environments. The need for them to safely grasp unknown objects without damaging them becomes crucial. Humans achieve this by sensing and quickly responding by adjusting their grasping force. Similarly, effective grasp acquisition in robots requires compliant interaction strategies that can adapt to uncertain object properties and adjust to any instabilities during manipulation.
  We present a geometry-aware force/torque-based contact estimation method for a coarse position-controlled gripper, combined with an adaptive admittance controller for safe grasp acquisition. The desired contact forces are estimated online to keep stable contact with objects of unknown properties. This enables compliant and stable grasps while avoiding excessive forces. Experiments with objects of different sizes, shapes, stiffnesses, and weights show that the proposed algorithm not only prevents slippage but also applies minimal force to safely grasp an object without causing excessive deformation.

</details>


### [230] [Parameter Sensitivity Analysis for Aerial LiDAR-Inertial Odometries in low-altitude flights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12837)
*Robert Milijas,Jose Ramiro Martinez-de Dios,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于LiDAR的SLAM算法（Cartographer和FAST-LIO2）中参数对无人机定位精度的影响，并提出了简化的调参建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR-based SLAM和LIO算法的性能严重依赖于场景、雷达和运动特征，通常需要密集且繁琐的调参过程来达到预期效果。

Method: 使用不同LiDAR在多样化环境中记录的无人机SLAM数据集，对FAST-LIO2和Cartographer进行穷举网格搜索；利用Pearson相关性和随机森林排列重要性分析评估各参数对绝对轨迹误差（ATE）的影响。

Result: 识别出了对算法性能影响最强的参数，并基于分析结果给出了简化的调参流程与建议；在94%的测试案例中，使用该调参建议得到的ATE与网格搜索的最优性能差距在5厘米以内。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析关键参数并提供针对性的调参建议，可以显著降低Cartographer和FAST-LIO2的调参难度，同时保持接近最优的定位精度。

Abstract: LiDAR-based SLAM (Simultaneous Localization and Mapping) and LIO (LiDAR-inertial odometry) algorithms are often used for precise navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles, especially during interactions with the aerial robot's environment. However, the performance of these algorithms is greatly dependent on the scenario, LiDAR, and robot motion characteristics, often requiring an intensive tuning process to achieve the desired performance. To aid these tuning efforts, this paper analyzes the influence on performance of the parameters of an EKF-based LIO algorithm (FAST-LIO2) and the LIO module of a graph-based SLAM algorithm (Cartographer) on aerial LiDAR SLAM datasets recorded using different LiDARs in low-to-moderate-altitude flights in diverse environments. The analysis is conducted on the absolute trajectory error (ATE) resulting from processing the datasets with the LIO algorithms configured with each combination of parameters obtained in an exhaustive grid search. The relationship between individual parameters and the ATE results is assessed using Pearson's correlation, while the influence of each parameter is assessed using random forest permutation importance analyses with random forest models trained to predict the resulting ATE values based on the choice of parameters. The performed analysis obtains for Cartographer and FAST-LIO2: i) the identification of parameters with stronger influence in performance, ii) a simplified tuning procedure, and iii) tuning recommendations. Using the proposed tuning recommendations, both algorithms obtain on the analyzed datasets ATE values within 5 cm to the optimal performance found in the grid search procedure in 94% of the analyzed cases.

</details>


### [231] [A Multi-Vehicle Dataset with Camera, LiDAR, and Radar Sensors and Scanned 3D Models for Custom Auto-Annotation using RTK-GNSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12871)
*Philipp Berthold,Bianca Forkel,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自动驾驶感知算法的新型数据集，提供目标车辆的高精度3D扫描模型、位姿及连续运动学参考，以深入评估测量原理及复杂环境效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自动驾驶数据集多采用语义分割、点云关联或边界框标注，难以深入评估传感器的测量原理及遮挡、反射等复杂物理效应，因此需要更精细的动态环境参考数据。

Method: 通过RTK-GNSS获取连续运动学参考和位姿，结合包含车辆表面法向量的3D扫描模型，记录单目标及多目标场景，从而在任意时间点完整掌握传感车辆周围的动态状态。

Result: 构建了一个包含七辆目标车辆的测数据集，能够推导出用户自定义粒度的后续参考格式，并有效用于评估遮挡和反射等测量效应。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过提供高精度的3D模型和连续运动学参考，为自动驾驶中传感器测量原理及环境特效的深入评估提供了一个强大的基础工具。

Abstract: Datasets are a crucial element in the development of perception algorithms. They relate sensor measurement data to annotated reference information and allow for the deduction of sensor and object characteristics. In autonomous driving, the reference data commonly consist of semantic image segmentation, point-wise associations, or bounding box annotations. The dataset proposed in this work, however, aims to dig deeper into the evaluation of measurement principles and provides scanned 3D models of all vehicles together with a pose and continuous kinematics reference obtained by RTK-GNSS. Combined, the state of the complete dynamic surrounding of the sensor vehicle is known for any point in time. Subsequent reference formats can be easily computed in user-defined granularity. This dataset involves single-object and multi-object recordings with seven target vehicles. In particular, measurement effects such as occlusion, as well as reflections, can be evaluated, as the normals of the shape of the target vehicles are known. We describe the dataset, discuss the technical background of its development, and briefly present exemplary evaluations.

</details>


### [232] [From Transportation to Manipulation: Enabling Grasping in Magnetic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12883)
*Lara Bergmann,Noah Greis,Cedric Grothues,Lisa-Marie Weigelt,Klaus Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Gripper MagBot，这是一种低成本的、集成夹爪的6自由度磁悬浮（MagLev）操作器，旨在无需额外机器人手臂的情况下直接在MagLev系统上进行抓取和操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的MagLev系统应用几乎完全集中在运输上，其操作能力尚未得到充分利用。为了在不增加昂贵处理设备（如工业机械臂）的前提下实现直接抓取和操作，需要开发集成化的低成本操作工具。

Method: 提出了一种名为Gripper MagBot的并行6自由度操作器，它集成了1自由度夹爪并机械耦合了三个MagLev动子。支持默认和单轨两种操作模式，可通过对接站自主装卸，并使用逆运动学控制器进行了仿真和真实硬件验证。

Result: 成功在仿真和真实硬件平台上通过逆运动学控制器展示了Gripper MagBot的抓取与放置（pick-and-place）应用，证明了其在MagLev系统上进行物料操作的可行性。

Conclusion: Gripper MagBot有效地扩展了磁悬浮系统在多品种、小批量制造中的操作能力，提供了一种低成本、可自主重构的物料搬运与抓取解决方案。

Abstract: Magnetic levitation (MagLev) systems have great potential for application in high-mix, low-volume manufacturing due to their scalability and flexibility, enabling highly reconfigurable in-machine material flow. However, their manipulation capabilities remain largely unexploited, as current applications almost exclusively focus on transportation. To enable grasping and manipulation directly on MagLev systems without requiring additional costly handling equipment, such as industrial robot arms, we present the Gripper MagBot, a low-cost parallel 6-DoF manipulator with an integrated 1-DoF gripper that mechanically couples three MagLev movers. The Gripper MagBot supports two operating configurations: a default mode and a single-track mode, selectable depending on the required stability and workspace footprint. To reconfigure a machine, the MagBot can be autonomously dropped off and picked up using a docking station. We showcase pick-and-place examples in simulation, as well as with the real Gripper MagBot using our inverse kinematics controller. CAD files, assembly instructions, a component list, and videos are available at https://sites.google.com/view/gripper-magbot.

</details>


### [233] [Before the Tipping Point: Force-Guided Active Perception for Shape-Agnostic Estimation of 3D Centers of Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12894)
*Steven M. Hyland,Jing Xiao,Cagdas D. Onal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于力的机器人操作方法，仅通过单次亚临界倾翻实验，即可在未知物体形状和质量分布的情况下，精确估计其质心高度和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当物体无法抓取、几何形状不规则或质量分布不均时，估计其3D质心极具挑战性。现有的方法缺乏对未知物体的通用性和安全性。

Method: 机器人机械臂通过施加准静态的提升推拉循环运动（push-retract cycle），利用力-角度测量值记录倾翻过程中的物体轨迹来识别参数，同时引入安全余量机制防止物体过度倾翻。

Result: 实验验证表明，该方法在各种未知物体上的质量、质心高度和倾翻角度的相对误差均低于5.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在适当的安全阈值下进行可靠的3D惯性参数估计是可行的，为之前难以处理的复杂物体的非抓取操作和机器人抓取提供了支持。

Abstract: Estimating the 3D center of mass of unknown objects is challenging when grasping is infeasible, geometry is irregular, or mass distribution is uneven. We present a force-based method that estimates CoM height and mass from a single sub-critical tipping experiment by a robot manipulator. The robot applies a quasistatic elevated push and retract motion, using force-angle measurements recorded during tipping to identify parameters from the object trajectory. Our proposed push-retract cycle mitigates frictional bias, enabling generalized fitting. We experimentally validate our method using a robot manipulator with a six-axis force torque sensor on varying types of objects without prior shape information and without specific models. We also propose a method to prevent toppling, keeping the object in a sub-critical tipping regime by leveraging a safety margin. In experimental studies, our method recovers mass, CoM height, and toppling angle with relative errors below 5.0 percent across all unknown objects. This work demonstrates reliable 3D inertial parameter estimation under proper safety thresholds in tipping. Our proposed method informs and enables reliable non-prehensile manipulation and robotic grasping of challenging objects that were previously infeasible.

</details>


### [234] [Robust Underwater Grasping of Sloped Objects with a Waterproof Passive Adaptive Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12927)
*Jooyoung Hong,Daewon Hong,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种防水的被动自适应夹爪，结合了局部和全局自适应能力，用于解决水下及日常环境中抓取倾斜、不对称及湿润物体时的滑动和滑移难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行夹爪在抓取倾斜、不对称物体时易产生扭矩驱动的滚动和剪切滑移，而在水下或潮湿环境中摩擦力的大幅降低使这一挑战更加严峻，现有技术难以满足日常环境中的水下稳定抓取需求。

Method: 设计了一种完全防水的夹爪，集成了用于在倾斜表面上进行全局自适应的被动旋转关节，以及用于局部表面适应的被动变刚度垫片。

Result: 在干燥和水下条件下对各种圆柱形和圆锥形物体的实验表明，与刚性垫片和固定关节基线相比，该设计展现出更强的抓取能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该提议的设计提供了一种实用且稳健的解决方案，能够成功实现对各种日常物体的稳定水下操作，有效填补了当前日常生活应用中水下机器人抓取的技术空白。

Abstract: Robust grasping of everyday objects remains challenging for parallel-jaw grippers, particularly when handling sloped or asymmetric items that induce torque-driven rolling and shear slip. These challenges become even more severe in domestic environments such as kitchens, where objects are often wet or submerged, drastically reducing friction between the gripper and the object. To address these issues, we present a waterproof passive adaptive gripper that combines local and global adaptability for high-performance grasping in the submerged environments. The proposed gripper features a fully waterproof design that integrates a passive rotational joint for global self-alignment on sloped surfaces and passive variable-stiffness pads for local surface adaptation. Experiments in both dry and underwater conditions on various cylindrical and conical objects demonstrate superior holding capability and grasp robustness compared to rigid-pad and fixed-joint baselines. The proposed design offers a practical and robust solution that successfully enables stable underwater manipulation of diverse everyday objects, effectively addressing a critical gap in current underwater robotic grasping for daily-life applications.

</details>


### [235] [ARC: Autonomous Robotics Compliance A Three-Layer Governance Architecture for Deployed Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12932)
*Tord Eide,Einar Holt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自主机器人系统的治理框架，包含一个三层合规架构（ARC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决自主机器人系统在安全性、认知能力和运营合规性方面日益增长的监管和治理需求。

Method: 引入了一个三层合规架构（ARC），具体通过模型安全验证、认知认证基准和运营授权标准来实现。

Result: 建立了一套结构化的治理机制，能够有效对自主机器人系统进行安全验证、能力认证与授权管理。

Conclusion: 该治理框架及其三层架构为确保自主机器人系统的安全可靠运行提供了切实可行的标准和实现路径。

Abstract: Proposed governance framework for autonomous robotic systems, introducing a three-layer compliance architecture (ARC) instantiated through model safety validation, cognitive certification benchmarks, and operational authorization standards.

</details>


### [236] [A Robot Among People:From Social Imitation to the Social Becoming of Human Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12937)
*Victor Tuan Vu Pham,Judith Dörrenbächer,Thomas H. Weisswange,Marc Hassenzahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的人机交互视角，认为公共空间中的机器人不应被设计为解决冲突或提高效率的“社会化代理”，而应作为促进人类群体共同体验、规范和认同的“关系催化剂”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统机器人设计中的“解决方案主义”陷阱（即将机器人视为修复人类群体功能失调的工具），探索一种将机器人理解为共享环境中情境化元素的全新设计思路。

Method: 基于先前关于“机器人街道家具”的研究，提出设计故意受限的、面向群体的实体，而非模仿人类社会性或追求高度自治与智能。

Result: 通过将机器人定位为一种“关系能力”，它们能够在不需要作为“我们中的一员”深度社交的情况下，激发人们围绕关怀、规范和身份认同进行群体层面的意义建构。

Conclusion: 应当根据人类共同生存与发展的需求来设计机器人，而不是照搬人类形象或单纯追求实用性。

Abstract: Robots designed to mediate human groups often fall into the solutionist trap: they are framed as sociable agents that fix problems such as conflict, disengagement, or lack of coordination. We suggest a different way of thinking. Rather than discrete agents, robots can be understood as situated elements of shared environments; catalysts and carriers of group experience whose meaning emerges through how people position, interpret, and interact with them. From this perspective, robots are not there to repair some ostensible dysfunctionality, but to enable group-level sense-making around care, norms, and identity. Our prior work on robotic street furniture suggests that this does not happen by imitating human sociality but by taking the shape of deliberately constrained, group-facing entities that happen and act for \textit{us} without being socially entangled as one of us. We thus understand robots in public spaces not in terms of autonomy or intelligence, but as a relational capacity. This implies designing robots not in our image or for our utility, but grounded in our needs in being and becoming together.

</details>


### [237] [Distributed Stochastic Optimal Control for Pattern-Oriented Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12959)
*Qingrui Zhang,Chenghao Yu,Feng Xue,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯随机场（GRF）的随机最优控制框架，用于解决模式导向的群体智能在几何控制、自组织和动态环境安全导航方面面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模式导向群体在处理几何控制、自组织以及动态环境中的安全导航时，面临着多方面的挑战，缺乏一个能够处理环境不确定性、非凸约束和异构动态的统一框架。

Method: 将GRF扩展到时间域，将集体协调转化为贝叶斯推断任务；开发了基于无迹变换的不确定性和安全感知防碰撞模块；引入了密度引导的模式控制，将几何图形编码为隐式密度场以实现分布式自愈和弹性重构。

Result: 通过多蒙特卡洛仿真验证了该框架的模型无关性（适用于四旋翼和固定翼UAV），并通过室内15个四旋翼和室外4个四旋翼的实际部署验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该基于GRF的随机最优控制框架能够使多智能体群体在真实世界的动态环境中实现可靠的几何模式转换和具安全意识的导航。

Abstract: While offering significant promise for diverse applications, pattern-oriented swarms encounter multifaceted challenges in geometric control, self-organization, and safe navigation through dynamic environments. In this paper, we present a GRF-based stochastic optimal control framework to address these challenges within a unified probabilistic architecture. By extending the GRF into the temporal domain, the proposed framework casts collective coordination as a Bayesian inference task, enabling swarms to accommodate environmental uncertainty, satisfy non-convex constraints, and reconcile heterogeneous dynamics across diverse platforms. We develop an uncertainty- and safety-aware collision avoidance module for navigation in the presence of stochastic obstacle motion. The unscented transform is employed to propagate state uncertainty for both dynamic obstacles and neighboring agents, yielding principled confidence bounds for collision avoidance. In addition, density-guided pattern control is introduced, which encodes geometric patterns as implicit density fields. This representation decouples pattern specification from explicit agent-to-target assignments, thereby facilitating intrinsic self-healing and elastic reconfiguration in a distributed manner. The proposed framework is extensively evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations across diverse scenarios. Its model-agnostic nature is demonstrated on both quadrotor and fixed-wing UAV swarms, highlighting its generalizability across platforms with heterogeneous dynamics. Finally, the efficacy and robustness of the proposed method are validated through indoor experiments with a 15-quadrotor swarm and outdoor deployments involving 4 custom-built autonomous quadrotors. These experiments substantiate the proposed framework's capacity to maintain reliable geometric pattern transitions and safety-aware navigation within real-world environments.

</details>


### [238] [Comfort by Construction: Adaptive, Comfort-Bounded Action Spaces for Learned Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13011)
*Anna Rothenhäusler,Daniel Jost,Raghu Rajan,Faris Janjos,Oliver Scheel,Andreas Look,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应动作参数化方法和PufferDrive-Editor工具，解决了数据驱动驾驶模拟器中由于缺乏加速度和加加速度约束而导致的强化学习策略产生不舒适的急转急停（“网格崩溃”）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的驾驶模拟器生成的强化学习策略常通过不切实际的急转弯来夸大安全指标，而简单地强制施加舒适度边界会导致静态网格饱和并破坏精细控制。

Method: 通过闭式求逆横向加加速度约束，提出了一种在每一步重新离散化网格的自适应动作参数化方法；同时开发了PufferDrive-Editor用于审计运动学和编写场景。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集和人工设计的绕桩场景中，该自适应模型将舒适度违规率保持在1%以下，同时在可导航性上优于裁剪网格和直接加加速度基线。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了驾驶模拟器中的控制限制与舒适度冲突问题，实现了人类级别的平稳驾驶策略，且具备优秀的导航性能。

Abstract: Data-driven driving simulators command accelerations and steering rates from a fixed grid without constraining the realized accelerations and jerks. As a result, reinforcement-learning policies inflate safety metrics through abrupt, last-second maneuvers that lie far outside the range of human driving and would be unacceptable to occupants of a real vehicle, so the metrics measure simulator permissiveness rather than policy quality. Enforcing comfort bounds naively is not enough: lateral limits shrink quadratically with speed, so clamping a static grid saturates it and destroys fine-grained control ("grid collapse"). We propose an adaptive action parameterization that rediscretizes the grid at every step to span exactly the per-step feasible control set, via closed-form inversion of the lateral-jerk constraint. We further present PufferDrive-Editor, a browser-based tool to audit realized kinematics and author kinematically challenging scenes. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset and a hand-authored slalom, our adaptive model holds comfort violations below 1% while outperforming clipped-grid and direct-jerk baselines in navigability.

</details>


### [239] [Dynin-Robotics: Omnimodal Unified Diffusion Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13053)
*Hoeun Lee,Jaeik Kim,Jusang Oh,Jinhyeok Kim,Geon Choi,Hyeonggeun Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dynin-Robotics，一种基于共享轨迹模型的机器人策略学习框架，利用全模态掩码扩散骨干网将语言、视觉观测、目标和动作统一为离散token，实现了视觉目标、动力学预测与动作生成的协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件的机器人策略需要目标结果和动作依赖的场景变化表征，通过将视觉目标和动力学预测引入动作生成与选择，提升机器人在复杂任务中的泛化能力和测试时扩展能力。

Method: 基于Dynin-Omni（全模态掩码扩散骨干网），通过改变条件和目标跨度，用同一模型学习动作预测、状态预测、目标预测和轨迹重建；在48个Open X-Embodiment数据集的约133万条轨迹上进行持续预训练，并采用优化的块并行实现加速动作解码。

Result: 在VLABench、LIBERO及LIBERO-Plus基准测试中表现出竞争力，在Franka Research 3机器人四个操作条件下的平均成功率达到78.4%，且模型端动作解码速度较基础实现提升高达29.2倍。

Conclusion: 共享轨迹模型为学习互补的机器人目标以及在控制过程中组合其预测结果提供了一个有效的通用接口，显著提升了机器人的适应能力和策略性能。

Abstract: Visual goal and dynamics prediction can provide language-conditioned robot policies with both a target outcome and a representation of action-dependent scene changes. We bring these predictions into action generation and selection through a shared trajectory model. Dynin-Robotics implements this formulation on Dynin-Omni, an omnimodal masked-diffusion backbone, representing language, visual observations, goals, and actions as discrete tokens. By varying conditioning and target spans, the same model learns action prediction, action-conditioned next-observation prediction, terminal goal-state prediction, and trajectory-to-instruction reconstruction. These interfaces support test-time scaling through goal prediction, action-candidate evaluation, and joint refinement of action and future-state predictions. We continually pretrain the model on approximately 1.33 million trajectories from 48 Open X-Embodiment datasets and adapt it separately to downstream domains. On two VLABench tasks, robot pretraining improves adaptation within a fixed Stage-2 step budget, and the full objective mixture improves shifted-instruction success over Policy-only post-training under the same coupled decoder. Combining goal guidance with joint action-next-state denoising further improves shifted-instruction success over action-only decoding; the benefit depends on how the predictions are composed. Dynin-Robotics achieves competitive performance on LIBERO and zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, together with a 78.4% average success rate across four manipulation conditions on a Franka Research 3 robot. An optimized block-parallel implementation accelerates model-side action decoding by up to 29.2x relative to the base implementation under the reported profiling setup. These results support shared trajectory modeling as a common interface for learning complementary robot objectives and composing their predictions during control.

</details>


### [240] [ASTRIL-MPC: Autonomous Traversal Framework of Articulated Tracked Robots with Language-Guided Neural-Kinematic MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13083)
*Zhenfeng Gan,Yanbo Chen,Lirong Che,Junbo Tan,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASTRIL-MPC，一个用于铰接式履带机器人（ATRs）自主通过复杂地形的语言引导神经运动学模型预测控制框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市搜救中，铰接式履带机器人穿越接触密集环境（如楼梯）时面临机器人-地形交互复杂、难以解析建模以及可靠自主性不足的挑战。

Method: 结合了学习型运动学模型（预测短视界任务状态增量）、带有严格可行性约束的多目标非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），以及通过安全检查接口提出权重和边界调整的大语言模型（LLM）。

Result: 相比非自适应NMPC和PPO基线，ASTRIL-MPC的综合通行质量评分分别提升了高达71%和67%，同时在下行过程中消除了可测量的碰撞冲击。

Conclusion: 学习型运动学、基于优化的规划和语言引导的重新调谐相结合，能够为铰接式履带机器人带来数据高效且鲁棒的自主性。

Abstract: In urban search and rescue, articulated tracked robots (ATRs) must traverse structured but contact-rich environments such as stairwells and cluttered building interiors. Reliable autonomy remains challenging because robot-terrain interaction (RTI) is hybrid and discontinuous, and effective flipper-track coordination is difficult to model analytically. We present ASTRIL-MPC, a language-guided neural kinematics model predictive control (MPC) framework for autonomous traversal. A learned kinematics model predicts short-horizon task-state increments from a height sequence and recent trajectories; NMPC plans with multi-objective costs and strict feasibility constraints; and a large language model (LLM) proposes bounded updates to selected weights and bounds through a safety-checked interface with range clipping, rate limiting, and consistency checks. The compiled predictor enables a full control cycle within 100 ms. Across three traversal tasks and a multi-height generalization setting, ASTRIL-MPC improves an aggregate traversal-quality score by up to 71% over a non-adaptive NMPC and by 67% over a PPO baseline, while eliminating measurable collision impacts during descent. These results indicate that combining learned kinematics, optimization-based planning, and language-guided retuning yields data-efficient and robust autonomy for articulated tracked robots.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Abstract homogeneous chains: a Lyapunov framework for high-order sliding modes in multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12250)
*Rodrigo Aldana-López*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一个用于多智能体系统中任意阶滑模算法的Lyapunov框架，实现了全局有限时间稳定性，并提出了递归优化增益建议程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有动态平均一致性和分布式微分结果中的多项空白，包括缺乏高阶增益建议规则以及收敛性通常局限于局部的问题。

Method: 引入抽象齐次链（一类具有共同凸性和齐次性特性的非线性误差系统），建立全局有限时间稳定性，构造齐次Lyapunov函数，并推导递归的基于优化的增益建议程序。

Result: 实现了任意阶的全局有限时间收敛，为EDCHO和分布式微分提供了递归数值优化增益建议规则，并提出了一种用于多领导者仿射形成跟踪的任意阶全局有限时间收敛观测器。

Conclusion: 该工作成功建立了一个通用的Lyapunov框架，能够系统性地解决多智能体系统中高阶滑模算法的稳定性分析与增益设计问题。

Abstract: This work develops a Lyapunov framework for a broad class of arbitrary-order sliding-mode algorithms in multi-agent systems. We introduce abstract homogeneous chains, a class of nonlinear error systems characterized by common convexity and homogeneity properties. For this class, we establish global finite-time stability for arbitrary order, construct a homogeneous Lyapunov function, and derive a recursive optimization-based gain-proposal procedure. The framework addresses several gaps in existing dynamic average consensus and distributed differentiation results: it provides a recursive numerical optimization-based gain-proposal procedure for EDCHO at arbitrary order, extends REDCHO convergence from local to global, and provides arbitrary-order numerical gain-proposal rules for leader-follower distributed differentiation, previously available only at first order. It also provides a new arbitrary-order observer for multi-leader affine formation tracking with global finite-time convergence.

</details>


### [242] [The Iterative Equivariant Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12328)
*Pieter van Goor,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了迭代等变滤波器（IterEqF），这是一种结合了迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波器（IterEKF）和等变滤波器（EqF）优点的新型状态估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将IterEKF在非线性加权最小二乘问题上的迭代修正优势与EqF在利用李群和齐次空间底层对称性方面的优势相结合，从而为导航问题提供高性能的状态估计方案。

Method: 推导了IterEqF更新步骤的迭代过程，利用该方法固有的非线性自然实现滤波器协方差在新品坐标系中的“重置”。

Result: 通过移动机器人的基于距离的定位的蒙特卡罗仿真，证明了该方法相对于标准EqF性能的提升，特别是在瞬态收敛阶段。

Conclusion: IterEqF成功结合了IterEKF和EqF的特性，在处理导航问题和状态估计时表现出优于标准EqF的高性能。

Abstract: This paper presents the iterative equivariant filter (IterEqF). The iterative extended Kalman filter (IterEKF) replaces the standard EKF correction step with an iterative correction step that is the Gauss-Newton solution to a nonlinear weighted least squares problem. The standard equivariant filter (EqF) exploits and respects the underlying symmetry of state estimation problems posed on homogeneous spaces and Lie groups. The motivation behind the IterEqF is to combine the features of both the IterEKF and the EqF, thus leading to a high-performance state estimation solution that is well suited to navigation problems. This paper derives the iteration procedure for the IterEqF update step and shows how the intrinsic nonlinearity of the approach naturally results in a `reset' of the filter's covariance into new coordinates. Monte-Carlo simulations of range-based localisation for a mobile robot demonstrate the improvement in performance relative to a standard EqF, especially during the transient convergence.

</details>


### [243] [e-Traceroute: Physically traceable electricity routing for carbon-free energy utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12348)
*Shiu Mochiyama,Ryo Takahashi,Yoshihiko Susuki*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 解析错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析错误

Method: 解析错误

Result: 解析错误

Conclusion: 解析错误

Abstract: Maximizing the self-consumption of residential photovoltaic generation is a promising pathway to meet urgent decarbonization targets for 2030 (and even for 2035). A viable solution is to create a sharing economy for idle battery capacity within a community. To achieve this, utilizing shared physical assets necessitates complete physical traceability of power flows---the capability to strictly trace the ownership of stored energy among multiple participants. Furthermore, physical traceability is an essential function for demonstrating the use of carbon-free energy resources. Such tracing is impossible in conventional systems owing to two fundamental limitations: the mixing of power flows in a common bus and the decoupling of power and information delivery. This study presents a novel physical-layer technology, called e-Traceroute, that overcomes these limitations and realizes physically traceable electricity exchanges. Specifically, physically distinguishable power routing and data transmission are unified over the same power lines. Prototyping experiments demonstrate successful integration of power transfers and information transactions, validating that the proposed system serves as a physical foundation for a reliable and scalable sharing economy that drives bottom-up decarbonization.

</details>


### [244] [Controlled Islanding and Reconnection with Stability Guarantees via Submodular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12335)
*Shiyu Cheng,Andrew Clark*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模网络化动态系统中的两阶段控制孤岛化与重连问题，旨在通过隔离不稳定区域并在之后恢复连通性来保证系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模网络化系统中，局部故障会传播并威胁整体稳定性。现有的孤岛化策略往往割裂了系统连通性，缺乏系统性的两阶段（孤岛化与重连）优化框架及严格的理论保证。

Method: 利用Lyapunov理论推导两个阶段的稳定性充分条件；通过构造增广图和拟阵（matroid）理论来刻画孤岛与重连决策；将优化问题转化为等价的非递减超模（supermodular）形式，并开发了带有反例求精的局部搜索算法。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的方法在求解质量和计算性能上均表现优异，并能有效处理不同的故障位置，优于传统的混合整数线性规划基准方法。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了一个基于图形拟阵和超模优化的两阶段控制孤岛化与重连理论框架，为复杂网络系统的抗故障恢复能力提供了有效的算法保证。

Abstract: In large-scale networked dynamical systems, local faults or disturbances may propagate through the interconnections and compromise the stability of the entire network. Partitioning the network into disjoint subsystems, or islands, limits this propagation by isolating unstable portions of the network from those that remain stable. This paper studies a two-stage controlled islanding and reconnection problem. In the first stage, the network is partitioned into stable and unstable islands. In the second stage, selected stable islands are reconnected to recover connectivity lost during islanding while satisfying stability requirements. We derive Lyapunov-based sufficient conditions for both stages. To address the coupling between the islanding and reconnection decisions, we construct an augmented graph on which the bases of a graphic matroid induce exactly the admissible islanding and reconnection pairs. We further show that the resulting optimization problem admits an equivalent nonincreasing supermodular formulation. Based on this structure, we develop a local-search algorithm with counterexample refinement and establish a performance guarantee for its inner loop with fixed sample sets. Numerical studies on linear networked systems illustrate the proposed procedure, examine its sensitivity to fault locations, and compare the proposed method with a mixed-integer linear programming benchmark in terms of solution quality and computational performance.

</details>


### [245] [Adaptive Agent Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12486)
*Raj Kiriti Velicheti,Subhonmesh Bose,Tamer Başar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体在一般非马尔可夫环境中，利用离线数据优化状态转移核与状态反馈控制策略的双层智能体设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决智能体在非马尔可夫环境中如何通过自由选择转移核并结合离线观测与动作数据来优化控制策略的问题。

Method: 利用软Q学习算法（soft Q-learning）分析其在一般环境下的收敛性，并在部分可观测马尔可夫决策问题（POMDPs）中，通过零阶（zero-th order）和贝叶斯优化技术分析参数化转移核设计的收敛性质。

Result: 证明了软Q学习算法几乎必然收敛到由行为策略和所选核诱导的平稳平均所定义的软贝尔曼方程的不动点，并阐明了所得策略与最优策略之间的区别。

Conclusion: 该研究为非马尔可夫环境下的双层智能体设计和转移核优化提供了理论收敛性保障与算法分析框架。

Abstract: We consider an agent acting against a general non-Markovian environment. The agent maintains its agent states, but is free to choose a transition kernel across those states and optimize its state-feedback control policies. We study the bi-level agent design problem that optimizes the transition kernel and the policy it induces, given said kernel with offline data of observations and actions obtained via a behavioral policy. For general environments, we show that a soft $Q$-learning algorithm converges almost surely to the fixed point of a soft Bellman equation defined by the stationary averages that the behavioral policy and the chosen kernel induce, and we delineate what separates the resulting policy from an optimal one. In partially observed Markov decision problems, we analyze convergence properties of parametrized transition kernel design via zero-th order and Bayesian optimization techniques.

</details>


### [246] [Computing at Sea: Floating and Offshore Data Centres as a Pathway to Sustainable AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12511)
*Cheng Siong Chin,Jianhua Zhang,M. Venkateshkumar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了海上和水上数据中心作为一种新兴替代方案，以应对人工智能迅速扩张带来的能源、冷却和环境限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统陆上数据中心在电力需求、冷却能力、土地使用和碳管理方面正接近物理极限，亟需寻找可持续的计算基础设施新模式。

Method: 通过分析海洋环境的自然优势、早期部署案例以及多能互补潜力，全面探讨了海上计算的工程设计、经济可行性、环境影响及监管治理。

Result: 海上数据中心能够利用海洋自然冷却能力、减少淡水依赖，并直接整合海上可再生能源，从而显著提高能源效率和运营可靠性。

Conclusion: 海上数据中心不仅是实验性创新，更是解决下一代计算增长在电力、冷却和可持续性方面系统性转变的关键途径。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of artificial intelligence is transforming data centres into one of the world's fastest-growing sources of electricity demand. As AI systems scale in size and capability, the physical infrastructure supporting computation is approaching critical limits in energy availability, cooling capacity, land use, freshwater consumption, and carbon management. Conventional land-based data centres are increasingly constrained by urban land competition, grid congestion, environmental pressures, and lengthy permitting processes, raising fundamental questions about where future computing infrastructure can sustainably exist. This article examines floating and offshore data centres as an emerging alternative model for digital infrastructure. By relocating computation to marine environments, offshore systems can exploit the ocean's natural cooling capacity, reduce freshwater dependence, and enable direct integration with offshore renewable energy resources such as wind, wave, and tidal power. Early deployments have demonstrated the potential for significantly improved energy efficiency and operational reliability compared with conventional facilities, while also opening new possibilities for distributed and resilient computing architectures. The article explores how offshore computing may reshape the future relationship between electrification, renewable energy, and large-scale AI infrastructure. It analyses the opportunities and trade-offs associated with marine deployment, including environmental impacts, engineering design challenges, economic feasibility, and regulatory governance. Rather than treating offshore data centres as experimental novelties, the article presents them as part of a broader systems-level transition in how society may power, cool, and sustain the next generation of computational growth.

</details>


### [247] [Aerial Non-Stop Trajectories Preserving the Equilibrium of Loads Suspended by Variable-Length Cables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12922)
*Antonio Franchi,Alberto Landi,Chiara Gabellieri*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了由变长电缆连接的空中载体悬挂刚性负载时，保持平衡的非停止轨迹运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现空中载体在维持负载受力不变的同时，通过内力运动和电缆长度调节来进行持续、平稳的非停止运动。

Method: 推导了沿固定方向实现规定载体位置轮廓的显式电缆长度规律，并基于几何和可行性条件分析了不同数量载体下的运动约束。

Result: 研究发现，变长电缆使双载体能够进行有界的非停止运动，但双载体的周期性水平非停止运动受到阻碍，而三个或更多载体可以通过显式几何条件构造实现。

Conclusion: 通过变长电缆和内力运动的协调，可以有效实现空中载体-负载系统的平衡保持与非停止轨迹规划，并通过数值仿真得到了验证。

Abstract: This work studies equilibrium-preserving non-stop trajectories of aerial carriers connected to rigid loads by variable-length cables. Internal-force motions vary the cable directions without changing the load wrench, while cable-length actuation shapes the radial realization of the carrier trajectories. We derive an explicit cable-length law for realizing prescribed carrier-position profiles along a fixed direction, with constant altitude as a relevant specialization. Under this constraint, a carrier stops exactly when its cable direction stops changing. This characterization reveals a distinction between unrestricted and level motion: variable cable lengths enable bounded non-stop motion with two carriers, whereas periodic level non-stop motion is obstructed for two carriers and constructively achieved for three or more under explicit geometric and feasibility conditions. Numerical simulations illustrate the constant-altitude construction.

</details>


### [248] [Very Exciting: Zero-Shot Model Predictive Control of Buildings via Excitation-Based Generalized Transfer Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12853)
*Fabian Raisch,Felix Koch,Zack Xuereb Conti,Christoph Goebel,Benjamin Tischler*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文研究了利用迁移学习技术减少建筑模型预测控制（MPC）中数据收集和建模的工作量，并提出了一种基于激发数据的广义模型预训练方法，无需目标建筑的特定数据即可实现优异的零样本控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的建筑节能模型预测控制（MPC）由于需要为每个建筑单独收集数据和训练模型而难以广泛应用；虽然迁移学习（TL）被用于减少数据需求，但现有研究通常只评估预测精度，而忽视了对下游控制性能的测试。

Method: 应用先进的迁移学习方法，通过使用基于激进激发（excitation-based）的运行源数据预训练广义模型，并在32个模拟目标建筑中进行零样本（无需微调）的MPC性能评估。

Result: 基于激发数据的广义模型在所有基准测试中表现出最强的控制性能，比在线线性模型MPC和PI控制器分别性能提升了6.4%和36.9%。

Conclusion: 该方法将强大的控制性能与跨建筑泛化能力相结合，且不需要任何目标建筑的特定数据，从而降低了MPC的建立成本并简化了其在建筑领域的广泛部署。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of data-driven, energy-efficient model predictive control (MPC) in buildings remains hindered by substantial effort to collect data and train models for individual buildings. Transfer learning (TL) has consequently gained increasing attention for target building modeling, as it reduces data requirements and modeling effort by reusing pretrained source models. However, these TL models are typically evaluated only on prediction accuracy in the target, without testing downstream control performance. To address this gap, we apply a state-of-the-art TL approach - pretraining a generalized model on multiple source buildings using standard operational data - within an MPC setup in a target building. We show that this approach is insufficient to achieve satisfactory control performance. As a solution, we introduce generalized models pretrained on excitation-based operational source data - purposefully probed inputs that explore the building's state-action space. For evaluation, we apply the generalized models via zero-shot (i.e., without fine-tuning) to 32 simulated target buildings and assess MPC performance. Our results show that excitation-based generalized models achieve the strongest control performance among all benchmarks, outperforming an online linear model-based MPC and a PI controller by 6.4% and 36.9%, respectively. By combining strong control performance with the ability to generalize across multiple buildings, without requiring any target-specific data, our approach reduces MPC setup cost and simplifies its widespread deployment in the building sector.

</details>


### [249] [End-to-End Battery Dispatch with Exact Rainflow Degradation via Mixed-Integer Differentiable Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12968)
*Eshagh Safarzadeh Ravajiri,Jan Drgona,Mahdi Mehrtash,Benjamin F. Hobbs*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督的混合整数可微分预测控制框架，利用新型可微分雨流计数层直接基于真实的电池退化物理特性训练神经网络策略，实现了电池储能系统的高效最优调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的雨流计数法能够准确量化电池退化，但其组合式、不可微的算法与凸优化和基于梯度的神经网络训练不兼容，限制了其实时大规模应用。

Method: 开发了一个新型可微分雨流层，结合了荷电状态极值处的精确梯度和增量变化的密集代理梯度，并构建了包含功率平衡、动态和模式排他性的混合整数可微分架构以及安全滤波器。

Result: 在多地区3650个真实电池日场景的评估中，该方法实现了相比传统混合整数求解器超过200倍到564倍的计算加速，性能差距仅为0.33%至3.33%，并保证了100%的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将精确的电池退化物理模型融入可微分神经网络训练，实现了毫秒级的推理速度和大规模实用部署，显著平衡了能量套利与电池寿命衰减。

Abstract: Optimal dispatch of battery energy storage systems requires balancing energy arbitrage against cycle-induced degradation, which is accurately quantified through rainflow cycle counting. However, rainflow's combinatorial, nondifferentiable algorithm is incompatible with both convex optimization and gradient-based neural network training. We present a self-supervised mixed-integer differentiable predictive control framework that trains neural policies directly on exact rainflow degradation through a novel differentiable rainflow layer combining exact gradients at state-of-charge extrema with dense proxy gradients on incremental changes, enabling stable end-to-end training while preserving true degradation physics. A mixed-integer differentiable architecture enforces power balance, dynamics, and mode exclusivity, with a safety filter guaranteeing constraint satisfaction. We evaluate the framework on 3,650 real battery-day scenarios (a 10-battery fleet over 365 days) spanning three utility regions (SDG&E California, Xcel Energy Colorado, APS Arizona) with diverse time-of-use pricing structures. A single-battery trained policy achieves a 0.35% performance gap on its training distribution with over 200x speedup, while fleet-wide training generalizes across all households and utility regions, achieving a 3.33% performance gap with 564x computational speedup (62 seconds vs. 9.7 hours) and 100% feasibility. The millisecond-scale inference reduces computation by over two orders of magnitude compared to mixed-integer solvers, enabling practical deployment at scale.

</details>


### [250] [Safe Stabilising Full-Order Affine Control Barrier Functions for Linear Systems (Extended)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12990)
*Faisal Lawan,Joaquin Carrasco,Lanlan Su*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对具有仿射约束的线性系统的安全且全局指数稳定的控制设计框架，解决了控制障碍函数安全滤波器引起的切换不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制障碍函数安全滤波器通过修改标称输入强制执行约束，但由此产生的切换即使在各个模式单独稳定时也可能导致闭环系统不稳定，因此需要一种能保证安全性和全局指数稳定性的设计框架。

Method: 利用障碍函数调谐固定滤波模式谱，推导出标称和滤波模式承认强共同二次Lyapunov函数的充分必要条件（基于频域正实性测试），并推导出显式共同存储函数，将线性矩阵不等式（LMI）综合条件简化为单个矩阵变量的可行性测试。

Result: 证明了滤波模式谱与对象、标称控制器及二次规划权重无关，得到了标称控制器保持安全滤波器不激活的显式条件，并通过双质量弹簧系统实例展示了该设计如何恢复安全性和全局指数稳定性。

Conclusion: 该设计框架成功解决了安全滤波器引起的稳定性问题，通过频域测试和显式增益区间提供了系统化的安全且全局指数稳定控制器的设计方法。

Abstract: Control barrier function safety filters enforce constraints by modifying a nominal input, but the resulting switching can destabilise the closed loop even when the nominal and filtered modes are individually stable. This paper presents a design framework for safe and globally exponentially stabilising controllers for linear systems with a single full-relative-degree affine constraint. We show that the filtered-mode spectrum is fixed by the barrier tuning and is independent of the plant, nominal controller, and quadratic-program weighting. This structure yields an explicit nominal controller for which the safety filter remains inactive everywhere. For a prescribed nominal controller, we prove that the nominal and filtered modes admit a strong common quadratic Lyapunov function if and only if the ratio of the nominal characteristic polynomial to the barrier polynomial is strongly strictly positive real. This equivalence characterises the existence of a common quadratic Lyapunov and provides a scalar frequency-domain test, along with an explicit interval of admissible gains. Building on these results, the extended analysis derives an explicit common storage function and reduces an existing LMI synthesis condition to a feasibility test in a single matrix variable. A flexible two-mass example explains a known instability mechanism and demonstrates how the proposed design restores safety and global exponential stability.

</details>


### [251] [An Adaptive Multi-Parameter ADMM Algorithm for Embedded MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12992)
*Alberto Zaupa,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应多参数ADMM变体，在识别出激活约束集后可实现局部超线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ADMM求解器在处理控制和优化问题时收敛速度受限，且通常只能达到较粗的精度，因此需要提高其收敛性能和求解效率。

Method: 引入一种自适应多参数ADMM变体，证明其局部超线性收敛性，并基于此实现了一个模型预测控制（MPC）求解器。

Result: 在仿真中，该算法在迭代次数上始终优于标准ADMM求解器（OSQP）；在挑战性基准测试中，其平均和最坏情况下的运行时间均表现出极具竞争力的性能，且不受限于粗精度。

Conclusion: 该自适应多参数ADMM方法能有效提升优化问题和MPC求解的收敛速度与运行性能，具有广阔的应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce an adaptive multi-parameter variant of ADMM and prove that it exhibits local superlinear convergence once the set of active constraints has been identified. In simulations, the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms OSQP, a standard ADMM solver, in terms of iteration count. We then implement an MPC solver based on our method and compare its runtime against a broader selection of state-of-the-art algorithms. Evaluations on challenging benchmark problems reveal that our approach delivers competitive performance both in terms of average and worst-case solve times, without being limited to coarse tolerances, as is typically the case for standard ADMM implementations and first-order methods.

</details>


### [252] [An Equivariant Filter for Spacecraft Nonlinear Relative Navigation with Range and Bearing Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13094)
*Gil Serrano,Bruno J. Guerreiro,Pedro Lourenço,Rita Cunha*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于等变滤波器（EqF）的相对导航方法，用于空间接近操作和自主交会对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对轨道动力学具有非线性、等变性以及半直积李群对称性，传统方法未能充分利用这些潜在的几何特性。

Method: 利用问题的潜在几何结构，将滤波器动力学提升到李群上，设计了等变滤波器（EqF）来估计追踪航天器与目标之间的相对位置和速度。

Result: 仿真实验验证了该滤波器的性能和有效性。

Conclusion: 利用李群对称性和等变滤波能够有效解决空间相对导航问题，展现出良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Relative navigation is a fundamental task in space proximity operations and autonomous rendezvous. The nonlinear relative orbital dynamics are equivariant and admit a semidirect product Lie group symmetry. This property is used to design an Equivariant Filter (EqF). The filter estimates the relative position and velocity between a chaser spacecraft and a target by lifting the filter dynamics to the group, while respecting the underlying geometry of the problem. Simulations demonstrate the filter's performance and effectiveness.

</details>

### [253] [Physics-informed denoising method for image reconstruction in quantitative low-field MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12966)
*Catarina Redshaw Kranich,Claudia Prieto,Christoph Kolbitsch,Felix Frederik Zimmermann*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种用于低场磁共振成像（MRI）的模块化展开端到端深度学习方法，可直接从k空间数据去噪重建定量参数图，且无需针对不同场强或序列重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低场MRI面临信噪比低和扫描时间长等挑战，而传统的深度学习方法需要大量高质量训练数据，这在低场应用中通常难以满足。

Method: 提出了一种由三个迭代应用子网络组成的模块化展开端到端深度学习方法，用于定量参数估计的正则化以及基于模拟信号曲线的信号估计。

Result: 该方法在0.55 T膝关节T2映射重建中优于其他传统和深度学习方法（中位误差小于4 ms），并且能够成功泛化应用于不同的磁场强度、序列和解剖结构。

Conclusion: 该方法为低场MR数据去噪提供了一种高效且灵活的解决方案，使定量低场MRI成为临床应用中可行的诊断工具。

Abstract: Low-field magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is becoming increasingly important for medical imaging because it can reduce healthcare costs while ensuring high diagnostic output. Nevertheless, quantitative imaging in low-field MRI faces challenges, such as low signal-to-noise ratio and long scan durations. Deep learning approaches have been proposed for image reconstruction to overcome these challenges. Still, deep learning often requires large high-quality training datasets which are usually not available for low-field applications. Here we propose a modular unrolled end-to-end deep learning method for the denoised reconstruction of quantitative parameter maps directly from k-space data for low-field MRI. It consists of three sub-networks that are iteratively applied. They are used for the regularization of the quantitative parameter estimation, as well as for the signal estimation that is based on simulated signal curves. It generalises well and can be applied to different field strengths and even different quantitative MR sequences without the need for new training data. We applied the presented method to noisy data of knees acquired at 0.55 T for the reconstruction of $T_2$-maps and compared it to other classical and deep learning methods. We also applied the proposed approach to $T_1$-mapping of knees at 72 mT and $T_2$-mapping of brains at 0.6 T. The presented approach outperforms the other reconstruction methods with a median difference below 4 ms to the ground truth $T_2$-map. Even though the network was trained with $T_2$-maps acquired at 0.55 T, it successfully denoised data acquired at different field strengths, sequences, and of different anatomies. As a result, the proposed network and its underlying method offer an efficient and flexible solution to denoise low-field MR data and make quantitative low-field MRI a feasible diagnostic tool for clinical applications.

</details>


### [254] [Recurrent Dynamic Range Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13135)
*Sebastian Dille,Keru Fu,S. Mahdi H. Miangoleh,Yağız Aksoy*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过循环神经网络逐步扩展图像动态范围的方法，用于恢复复杂场景的高动态范围（HDR）图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接重建复杂场景的全动态范围任务难度较大，以往方法表现受限，因此需要一种更易于学习且鲁棒的渐进式重建方案。

Method: 设计了一个循环神经网络，首先学习单曝光值的动态范围扩展，然后通过递归执行逐步增加动态范围；采用有界输出域、对抗损失以及用于反向传播的记忆重放（Memory Replay）机制来训练网络。

Result: 系统能够稳健地重建具有挑战性的长尾HDR场景，并展现出对强光源和高光区域强大的恢复能力。

Conclusion: 通过渐进式扩展动态范围并结合循环训练策略，该方法能够有效且逼真地从常规RAW图像中重建全HDR图像。

Abstract: We present an approach to progressively extend the highlights of an image. Instead of reconstructing the full dynamic range of a complex scene directly, we learn a simpler task first: We extend the dynamic range of an input image by a single exposure value. Once this is mastered, we retrieve the full HDR image for the scene by executing our network recurrently, progressively increasing the dynamic range of the input. Our formulation is agnostic to the input dynamic range and targets a bounded output domain. This enables us to use widely available RAW images for the reconstruction task and adapt adversarial losses to construct realistic images. By incorporating Memory Replay for backpropagation, we can train our network recurrently over multiple inference stages and reduce reconstruction errors. As a consequence, our system reconstructs challenging long-tailed HDR scenes robustly and shows powerful recovery of bright light sources and highlights.

</details>
